{"antonyms":[{"word":"early"},{"word":"mid"},{"word":"middle"}],"forms":[{"form":"later","tags":["comparative"]},{"form":"latest","tags":["superlative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"adj","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The seedlings appeared to be coming along nicely until a late frost killed them.","type":"example"},{"text":"The meeting is convened for late morning.","type":"example"},{"text":"In Southern U.S., \"evening\" is used for middle to late afternoon.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Near the end of a period of time."]},{"examples":[{"text":"It was getting late and I was tired.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Specifically, near the end of the day."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Late Latin is less fully inflected than Classical Latin.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Associated with the end of a period."],"tags":["not-comparable","usually"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The flowers were late in blooming because of the prolonged cold weather.","type":"example"},{"text":"Panos was so late that he arrived at the meeting after Antonio, who had the excuse of being in hospital for most of the night.","type":"example"},{"text":"The heavy snow made all the trains late.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Not arriving or occurring until after an expected time."],"synonyms":[{"word":"tardy"}]},{"examples":[{"text":"The power company suspended late fees during the pandemic.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Levied as a surcharge on a payment which has not arrived by a specified deadline."]},{"examples":[{"text":"I'm late, honey. Could you buy a test?","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Not having had an expected menstrual period."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Her late husband had left her well provided for.","type":"example"},{"text":"Mary was entitled to the crown by her late father’s testament.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Recently deceased, dead: used particularly when speaking of the dead person's actions while alive. (Generally must be preceded by a possessive or an article, commonly “the”; see usage notes. Can itself only precede the person's name, never follow it.)"],"tags":["euphemistic","not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"the late bishop of London","type":"example"},{"text":"the late administration","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Existing or holding some position not long ago, but not now; departed, or gone out of office."],"tags":["dated"]},{"examples":[{"text":"the late war","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Recent — relative to the noun it modifies."]},{"glosses":["Of a star or class of stars, cooler than the sun."],"topics":["astronomy","natural-sciences"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/leɪt/","tags":["General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/læɪt/","tags":["Australia","New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"/let/","tags":["Scotland"]},{"ipa":"/leːt/","tags":["Wales"]}],"synonyms":[{"word":"late"}],"upstream_line":217676,"upstream_offset":1055258865,"upstream_record_sha256":"cc92e72aab360b7b9096f0078e95dabd0509ec8b56fede5702446c93d93b1b15","word":"late"}
